  &#x20;Varadkar and Martin also condemned Israel's conduct in Gaza; Varadkar described Israel as "collective\[ly] punish\[ing]" Gaza for crimes done by Hamas, saying that it violates international law.

  [^cite_note-297]

  &#x20;Martin also condemned what he viewed as collective punishment, saying that while Israel has the right to defend itself against Hamas, "Two wrongs don't make a right."

  [^cite_note-298]

  &#x20;He further said that Israel must stay "within the parameters of [international humanitarian law](1093604.md)", and that Israel's call to evacuate northern Gaza was "deeply dangerous and impossible to implement".

  [^cite_note-299]

  [^cite_note-300]

  &#x20;On 14 February 2024, Taoiseach Varadkar along with Spanish Prime Minister Pedro Sánchez demanded in a joint letter to the [EU Commission president](24468.md) Ursula von der Leyen and the EU's [High Representative for Foreign Affairs and Security Policy](750942.md) Josep Borrell to assess whether Israel is complying with the obligations regarding human rights stipulated in the EU–Israel Association Agreement, and to take immediate measures in case of a breach in the agreement.

  [^cite_note-\:13-301]

-  [Italy](14532.md): Foreign Minister [Antonio Tajani](1767298.md) said the government condemned in the strongest terms the attacks on Israel; saying that "people's lives, the security of the region and the resumption of any political process are at risk." He also urged Hamas to "immediately stop this barbaric violence" and said that Italy supports "[Israel's right to exist](40229800.md) and defend itself".

  [^cite_note-302]

  &#x20;The [Prime Minister's](273741.md) office released a statement saying that the government was "closely following the brutal attack taking place in Israel" and condemned "in the strongest terms the ongoing terror and violence against innocent civilians" It also said it supported "Israel's right to defend itself."

  [^cite_note-\:4-112]

-  [Kosovo](17391.md): President [Vjosa Osmani](61979540.md) denounced Hamas' assault, labeling it a "terrorist attack against the innocent people of Israel," and expressed unwavering solidarity with the State of Israel.

  [^cite_note-303]

  [^cite_note-304]

  &#x20;Likewise, Prime Minister [Albin Kurti](4774301.md) unequivocally condemned the "terrorist attacks against Israel", adding that Kosovo stood in solidarity with those affected.

  [^cite_note-305]



Latvian President [Edgars Rinkēvičs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edgars_Rink%C4%93vi%C4%8Ds) with Israeli President Herzog in Jerusalem, 20 November 2023

-  [Latvia](17514.md): President [Edgars Rinkēvičs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edgars_Rink%C4%93vi%C4%8Ds) expressed his country's "unwavering support to Israel's legitimate right to defend itself against terrorists".

  [^cite_note-306]

  &#x20;Foreign Minister [Krišjānis Kariņš](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kri%C5%A1j%C4%81nis_Kari%C5%86%C5%A1) accused Hamas of using Palestinian civilians as [human shields](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_shield).

  [^cite_note-307]

-  [Liechtenstein](17810.md): Liechtenstein voted to condemn Hamas at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-auto4-39]

-  [Lithuania](17675.md): President [Gitanas Nausėda](58519561.md) said that the country unequivocally condemned Hamas attacks against civilians in Israel and said the country fully supported "Israel in these terrible hours", and expressed "condolences to the families of the victims". He also said that "Israel has the right to defend itself."

  [^cite_note-308]

  &#x20;The [Seimas](571739.md) unanimously adopted a resolution condemning Hamas' attacks.

  [^cite_note-309]

-  [Luxembourg](17515.md): Foreign Minister [Jean Asselborn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jean_Asselborn) condemned Hamas's attack, and underlined the need for a renewed international commitment to a two-state solution.

  [^cite_note-310]

-  [Malta](19137.md): The government called on the [United Nations Security Council](31956.md) to hold a meeting regarding the conflict following the attacks.

  [^cite_note-aj7oct-311]

-  [Moldova](19260.md): President [Maia Sandu](40796149.md) strongly condemned "Hamas' attack against Israeli innocent civilians".

  [^cite_note-312]

-  [Montenegro](20760.md): President [Jakov Milatović](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jakov_Milatovi%C4%87) condemned "the attack carried out by Hamas against Israel" and expressed "his condolences for the Israeli lives lost in these attacks".

  [^cite_note-313]

  [^cite_note-314]

-  [Netherlands](21148.md): Prime Minister [Mark Rutte](4321056.md) said he spoke with Prime Minister Netanyahu about the attack by Hamas on Israel and "told him that the Netherlands unequivocally condemns this terrorist violence and fully supports Israel's right to defend itself".

  [^cite_note-315]

  [^cite_note-\:4-112]

-  [North Macedonia](23564616.md): President [Stevo Pendarovski](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stevo_Pendarovski) condemned the "terrorist attacks launched against Israel and the killing of innocent civilians".

  [^cite_note-316]



Solidarity demonstration for Gaza in front of the [Norwegian Ministry of Foreign Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Norwegian_Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs) in [Oslo](22309.md) on 14 October 2023

-  [Norway](21241.md): Prime Minister [Jonas Gahr Støre](2926932.md) strongly condemned the attack against Israeli civilians, calling the situation "unprecedented". He said that "Israel has the right to defend itself against military attack" and that "it is important that the violence does not escalate".

  [^cite_note-317]

  &#x20;On 10 November 2023, Støre stated Israel's actions constituted a "violation of the international law of war".

  [^cite_note-318]

-  [Poland](22936.md): Foreign Minister [Zbigniew Rau](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zbigniew_Rau) condemned "in the strongest terms the ongoing attacks by Hamas against Israel".

  [^cite_note-319]

  [^cite_note-\:4-112]

-  [Portugal](23033.md): President [Marcelo Rebelo de Sousa](1652059.md) and Prime Minister [António Costa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ant%C3%B3nio_Costa) condemned Hamas's attacks as "unacceptable" and deserving of "strong condemnation".

  [^cite_note-320]

  &#x20;Costa later urged Israel to respect the civilian population in Gaza while defending itself.

  [^cite_note-321]

-  [Romania](25445.md): President [Klaus Iohannis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Klaus_Iohannis) strongly condemned the attack against Israel, adding that the country stood "in full solidarity with Israel in these terrible moments."

  [^cite_note-322]

-  [Russia](25391.md): President [Vladimir Putin](32817.md) expressed condolences to the families of deceased Israelis,

  [^cite_note-323]

  &#x20;expressed concern over the catastrophic increase of the number of civilians killed in Israel and Gaza,

  [^cite_note-324]

  &#x20;and called the conflict "a clear example of the failure of U.S. policy in the Middle East", accusing the U.S. of failing to consider the "fundamental interests of the Palestinian people". Putin also described the Hamas attack as "unprecedented in its cruelty" and affirmed Israel's right to defend itself, but called for a two-state solution to resolve the conflict.

  [^cite_note-325]

  &#x20;Russian UN diplomats later condemned both the Hamas attack on Israel and the Israeli "non-discriminatory shelling of peaceful neighborhoods in Gaza".

  [^cite_note-326]

  &#x20;Russia hosted a Hamas delegation led by [Mousa Abu Marzouk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mousa_Abu_Marzook) on 26 October, leading Israel to protest.

  [^cite_note-327]

  &#x20;\
  &#x20;Foreign Minister [Sergei Lavrov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sergei_Lavrov) said the "most reliable" solution for peace in Israel was the creation of a Palestinian state,

  [^cite_note-328]

  &#x20;and later added that the Israeli bombardment of Gaza was against international law.

  [^cite_note-329]

  &#x20;Russia voted against a resolution condemning Hamas at the [United Nations General Assembly](31957.md).

  [^cite_note-auto4-39]

  &#x20;As of 17 November, Russia had delivered 140 tons of humanitarian aid via plane to [El-Arish Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/El-Arish_Airport) for people in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-330]

  &#x20;On 28 December 2023, Lavrov said Russia's goal to "[de-Nazify](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Denazification)" Ukraine was similar to Israel's goals in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-331]

  -  [Chechnya](6095.md): The republic's head [Ramzan Kadyrov](647880.md) issued a statement supporting Palestine and expressing readiness to send in Chechen peacekeepers.

    [^cite_note-332]

    [^cite_note-333]

    &#x20;On 20 October 2023, Kadyrov described Israel's actions in Gaza as an act of [genocide](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Palestinian_genocide_accusation) against the Palestinian people.

    [^cite_note-334]

    [^cite_note-335]

-  [San Marino](27248.md): Secretary for Foreign and Political Affairs [Luca Beccari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luca_Beccari) condemned the attacks on Israel.

  [^cite_note-336]

-  [Serbia](29265.md): President [Aleksandar Vučić](13349267.md) expressed his support for Israel and condemned "the horrific attacks on Israel." He added that "the Jewish people have endured a history of suffering and Israel deserves to live in peace and security".

  [^cite_note-337]

-  [Slovakia](26830.md): Defence Minister [Martin Sklenár](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martin_Sklen%C3%A1r) condemned the Hamas attacks in the "strongest terms" and supported Israel's right to self-defense.

  [^cite_note-338]

-  [Slovenia](27338.md): Prime Minister [Robert Golob](70490527.md) condemned the Hamas attacks.

  [^cite_note-339]

  &#x20;Golob became more critical of Israeli actions in Gaza as the conflict continued, calling for an international movement to recognize a Palestinian state.

  [^cite_note-340]

  &#x20;In May 2025, President [Nataša Pirc Musar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nata%C5%A1a_Pirc_Musar) referred to the situation in Gaza as a "[genocide](75668427.md)".

  [^cite_note-341]

  &#x20;In July 2025, the government of Slovenia imposed an [arms embargo on Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arms_embargoes_on_Israel_since_2023).

  [^cite_note-342]

-  [Spain](26667.md): Prime Minister [Pedro Sánchez](43297512.md) strongly condemned what he called terrorism and demanded the immediate cessation of indiscriminate violence against the civilian population, likewise affirming Spain's standing commitment to regional stability.

  [^cite_note-343]

  [^cite_note-344]

  &#x20;Deputy prime minister [Yolanda Díaz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yolanda_D%C3%ADaz) called on the international community to put pressure on Israel to stop what she called a massacre in Gaza,

  [^cite_note-345]

  &#x20;while Minister of Social Rights [Ione Belarra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ione_Belarra) accused the EU and the US of "being complicit in Israel's war crimes" and called for Israel to be denounced before the [International Criminal Court](14880.md) because of what she identified as ongoing "planned [genocide](12441.md)" in the Gaza Strip against the Palestinian peoples.

  [^cite_note-346]

  [^cite_note-347]

  &#x20;In November 2023, Sanchez criticized Israel's bombardment of the Gaza Strip and called for an "immediate ceasefire". He promised to "work in Europe and in Spain to recognise the Palestinian state".

  [^cite_note-348]

  &#x20;On 23 November 2023, Prime Minister Sánchez along with Belgian Prime Minister Alexander De Croo held a [press conference](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Press_conference) at the [Rafah border crossing](10475618.md), emphasizing the importance of Israel's adherence to [international humanitarian law](1093604.md).

  [^cite_note-\:14-249]

  &#x20;\
  &#x20;On 26 January 2024, the Spanish government issued a statement celebrating the [International Court of Justice](14918.md)'s decision in regard of the [Application of the Convention on the Prevention and Punishment of the Crime of Genocide in the Gaza Strip (South Africa v. Israel)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/South_Africa_v._Israel_\(Genocide_Convention\)), calling on all parties "to respect and comply with these measures in their entirety".

  [^cite_note-349]

  &#x20;On 14 February, Prime Minister Sánchez along with Irish Taoiseach Leo Varadkar demanded in a joint letter to the [EU Commission president](24468.md) Ursula von der Leyen and the EU's [High Representative for Foreign Affairs and Security Policy](750942.md) Josep Borrell to assess whether Israel is complying with the obligations regarding human rights stipulated in the EU–Israel Association Agreement, and to take immediate measures in case of a breach in the agreement.

  [^cite_note-\:13-301]

  &#x20;In June 2024, Spain asked the International Court of Justice for permission to join South Africa's case.

  [^cite_note-350]

-  [Sweden](5058739.md): Foreign Minister [Tobias Billström](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tobias_Billstr%C3%B6m) condemned the attack against Israel, and said the government stood in solidarity with all civilians hurt by the attackers.

  [^cite_note-351]

-  [Switzerland](26748.md): The government condemned the attacks and asked for all parties to work toward a peaceful resolution. It also "underlined that de-escalation is the priority" and "called on those responsible to do everything possible to bring about a ceasefire and avoid a regional escalation".

  [^cite_note-352]

-  [Turkey](11125639.md): At a congress for his ruling [AK Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AK_Party) in [Ankara](802.md), President [Recep Tayyip Erdoğan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Recep_Tayyip_Erdo%C4%9Fan) called on Israelis and Palestinians to act with restraint and refrain from hostile acts that could exacerbate the situation.

  [^cite_note-353]

  [^cite_note-\:4-112]

  &#x20;Erdoğan later refused to use the word "terrorist" to describe Hamas and instead called them a "liberation group".

  [^cite_note-354]

  &#x20;Erdoğan said that Israel's bombing and [blockade of the Gaza Strip](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/October_2023_Israeli_blockade_of_the_Gaza_Strip) in retaliation for Hamas' attack was a disproportionate response amounting to a "massacre".

  [^cite_note-355]

  &#x20;On 25 October, Foreign Minister [Hakan Fidan](39996069.md) warned that an [Israeli ground invasion of the Gaza Strip](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_invasion_of_the_Gaza_Strip_\(2023%E2%80%93present\)) could turn into a massacre, saying that those supporting Israel's actions are "accomplices to its crimes".

  [^cite_note-356]

  &#x20;Turkey voted against condemning Hamas at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-auto4-39]

  &#x20;On 4 November, Turkey recalled its ambassador to Israel "in view of the unfolding humanitarian tragedy in Gaza caused by the continuing attacks by Israel against civilians, and Israel's refusal (to accept) a ceasefire."

  [^cite_note-turrecall-28]

  &#x20;In 2024, Turkish President Erdogan threatened Israel, stating that Turkey may enter Israeli territory just as it had done in [Libya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Turkish_intervention_in_Libya_\(2020%E2%80%93present\)) and [Nagorno-Karabakh](4695860.md).

  [^cite_note-357]

  [^cite_note-358]

-  [Ukraine](31750.md): Following the initial Hamas attack, the [Foreign Ministry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(Ukraine\)) said Ukraine strongly condemned the "terrorist attacks" against Israel and expressed its "support for Israel in its right to defend itself and its people."

  [^cite_note-359]

  [^cite_note-\:4-112]

  &#x20;On 9 October, [Ukrainian military intelligence](1406269.md) accused Russia of transferring Western-made weapons captured in Ukraine to Hamas to blame Ukraine for selling them.

  [^cite_note-360]

  &#x20;Following the [Al-Ahli Arab Hospital explosion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Ahli_Arab_Hospital_explosion), Ukraine's Foreign Ministry called for an "investigation of this tragedy" to bring perpetrators to justice, called on all sides to abide by international humanitarian law, and supported a two-state solution to the conflict.

  [^cite_note-361]



British Foreign Secretary [James Cleverly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cleverly) in Israel, 11 October 2023



UK Prime Minister [Rishi Sunak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rishi_Sunak) with Israeli Prime Minister [Benjamin Netanyahu](56472.md) in Tel Aviv, Israel, 19 October 2023



British Foreign Secretary [David Lammy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Lammy) with Netanyahu in Jerusalem, Israel, 14 July 2024

-  [United Kingdom](31717.md): Conservative Prime Minister [Rishi Sunak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rishi_Sunak) said he was shocked by the initial Hamas attacks against Israeli citizens. He added that "Israel has an absolute right to defend itself" and said UK officials were "in contact with Israeli authorities," and advised British nationals in Israel to "follow travel advice".

  [^cite_note-362]

  &#x20;An illumination of the [Israeli flag](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_flag) was projected onto the UK Prime Minister's residence at [10 Downing Street](226294.md) in London on 9 October.

  [^cite_note-363]

  &#x20;A statement issued by [Buckingham Palace](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Buckingham_Palace) said that [King Charles III](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/King_Charles_III) was "appalled" and condemned the "barbaric acts of terrorism in Israel" and that the King's "thoughts and prayers are with all of those suffering, particularly those who have lost loved ones, but also those actively involved as we speak".

  [^cite_note-364]

  &#x20;On 11 October 2023, Foreign Secretary [James Cleverly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cleverly) arrived in Israel in a show in solidarity, during which he was seen running for cover following an air raid alert while he was visiting the town of [Ofakim](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ofakim) later in the day.

  [^cite_note-365]

  &#x20;On 17 December 2023, [Foreign Secretary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foreign_Secretary_\(United_Kingdom\)) [David Cameron](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Cameron) backed a "sustainable ceasefire" differentiated from a "general and immediate ceasefire", called for more aid to reach Gaza, and called for the Israeli government to "do more to discriminate sufficiently between terrorists and civilians".

  [^cite_note-govuk-sustainable-366]

  &#x20;On 7 April 2024, [Foreign Secretary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foreign_Secretary_\(United_Kingdom\)) [David Cameron](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Cameron) said that UK support for Israel was not unconditional.

  [^cite_note-367]

  &#x20;The Conservative government was replaced by a Labour government led by [Keir Starmer](18681491.md) in July 2024. Starmer said "The humanitarian situation in Gaza is simply unacceptable".

  [^cite_note-368]

  [^cite_note-369]

  &#x20;Starmer issued a joint statement in May 2025 condemning Israel's [renewed offensive against Gaza](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/May_2025_Gaza_offensive). The statement called for Israel to immediately stop its military operations and to immediately allow humanitarian aid into Gaza. It condemned Israel's plan to ethnically cleanse the Gaza Strip as "abhorrent" and against international law. He said his government would take "concrete actions" if Israel continued its "egregious actions".

  [^cite_note-370]

  &#x20;Foreign Secretary [David Lammy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Lammy) called Israel's renewed offensive "morally unjustifiable". Referring to Israeli plans to ethnically cleanse the Gaza Strip, Lammy said "It is extremism, it is dangerous, it is repellent, it is monstrous, and I condemn it in the strongest possible terms".

  [^cite_note-371]

  &#x20;The UK is among 12 countries backing trade restrictions on illegal Israeli settlements in the occupied West Bank. In September 2026, Britain's foreign secretary said the UK would ban imports of goods from Israeli settlements, doing so in step with [France](5843419.md) and [Canada](5042916.md). A broader sanctions regime is also planned. Britain made clear it can no longer just condemn injustice and suffering — it has to act. It also stated that the occupation of the West Bank is illegal, and that London believes "ethnic cleansing" of Palestinians is happening in parts of the [West Bank](33209.md). The UK backs the two-state solution, which Israel rejects.

  [^cite_note-372]

*  [Scotland](26994.md): First Minister [Humza Yousaf](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Humza_Yousaf) condemned the attack by Hamas. Yousaf, whose wife is Palestinian, said that his parents-in-law were trapped in Gaza,

  [^cite_note-\:6-373]

  &#x20;adding that they had contacted them over the phone.

  [^cite_note-\:7-374]

  &#x20;In an interview with [Al Jazeera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Jazeera_English), he called for a humanitarian corridor to be established in Gaza to allow vital supplies in and people to leave. He also said that while he understood Israel's reasons to protect itself, he did not believe that the price should be the "collective punishment of two million people".

  [^cite_note-375]

  -  [England](9316.md)&#x20;

    - [London](17867.md): Mayor [Sadiq Khan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sadiq_Khan) said he had asked the UK government to support calls for humanitarian access to and from Gaza

      [^cite_note-376]

      &#x20;Khan urged Israel to exercise restraint, arguing that a [blockade of the Gaza Strip](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/October_2023_Israeli_blockade_of_the_Gaza_Strip) could lead to "suffering" among Palestinian civilians.

      [^cite_note-377]

  -  [Wales](69894.md): The [Welsh Parliament](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Welsh_Parliament) passed a [Plaid Cymru](53343.md) motion calling for an immediate ceasefire on 8 November after [the Labour-led Welsh government](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Second_Drakeford_government) abstained and gave its backbenchers a [free vote](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conscience_vote).

    [^cite_note-378]

*  [Vatican City](32408.md): [Pope Francis](1687680.md) called for peace between Israel and Palestine.

  [^cite_note-379]

  &#x20;Following the [Angelus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Angelus) prayer on 8 October, he called for a halt to the fighting, and said that "terrorism and war do not lead to any solution, but only to the death and suffering of so many innocent people", adding that "war is always a defeat".

  [^cite_note-vatican-380]

  &#x20;He later urged Hamas to release all its hostages, and voiced concern over Israel's imposition of a total siege on Gaza while saying that it was the "right of those who are attacked to defend themselves", referring to the Israeli military response.

  [^cite_note-381]

  &#x20;On 22 November, he stated the conflict had gone beyond war into terrorism, and warned Israel it was "forbidden to respond to terror with terror".

  [^cite_note-382]

  &#x20;On 31 March, during his [Easter](9325.md) address, he called for a ceasefire to the conflict and the return of all hostages in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-383]

### Oceania

-  [Australia](4689264.md): Foreign Minister [Penny Wong](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Penny_Wong) said that "Australia unequivocally condemns the attacks on Israel by Hamas including indiscriminate rocket fire on cities and civilians."

  [^cite_note-384]

  &#x20;[Prime Minister](24117.md) [Anthony Albanese](556745.md) also strongly condemned the attacks.

  [^cite_note-385]

  &#x20;In July 2025, Australia and several other Western countries issued a joint statement calling for an immediate end to the war in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-\:28-279]

  -  [New South Wales](21654.md): Premier [Chris Minns](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chris_Minns) condemned the attacks. He also condemned a pro-Palestinian and anti-Israeli rally in [Sydney](27862.md) where protesters chanted anti-Zionist slogans and lit flares.

    [^cite_note-386]

    &#x20;Minns also apologized for the arrest of a man who held an Israeli flag in the streets of Sydney, which police stated was for his own safety.

    [^cite_note-387]

    &#x20;and officially banned a planned second pro-Palestinian rally. The [Sydney Opera House](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sydney_Opera_House) was lit up in blue and white in tribute to Israel.

    [^cite_note-rallyban-388]

  -  [Queensland](59051.md): Premier [Annastacia Palaszczuk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Annastacia_Palaszczuk) condemned the attacks after it was announced that a pro-Palestinian rally would be taking place in [Brisbane](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brisbane).

    [^cite_note-389]

  -  [South Australia](26716.md): Premier [Peter Malinauskas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Malinauskas) condemned the attacks and shared photos on social media showing many landmarks in [Adelaide](1148.md) lit up in solidarity with Israel.

    [^cite_note-390]

  -  [Tasmania](29944.md): Premier [Jeremy Rockliff](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeremy_Rockliff) condemned the attacks.

    [^cite_note-391]

    &#x20;The [Tasman Bridge](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tasman_Bridge) in [Hobart](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hobart) was lit up in blue and white for three nights in solidarity with Israel.

    [^cite_note-392]

  -  [Victoria](4689460.md): Premier [Jacinta Allan](11169885.md) condemned the attacks from Hamas, which she labelled "terrorist attacks", and many landmarks in [Melbourne](17306237.md) lit up in solidarity with Israel.

    [^cite_note-393]

    &#x20;Initially, she did not label the violence as terrorism, a move that was highly criticized.

    [^cite_note-394]

  -  [Western Australia](33613.md): Premier [Roger Cook](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roger_Cook_\(politician\)) condemned the attacks and urged Western Australians against flying to the [Middle East](19323.md) to take up arms.

    [^cite_note-395]

-  [Fiji](10707.md): Prime Minister [Sitiveni Rabuka](516580.md) condemned the "terrorist attacks by Hamas which targeted innocent non-combatants" and reaffirmed Fiji's support for Israel's right to "defend itself and its people".

  [^cite_note-396]

  &#x20;In June 2025, Fiji voted against a resolution calling for a ceasefire at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-\:29-397]

-  [Kiribati](16674.md): Kiribati voted to condemn Hamas at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-auto4-39]

-  [Marshall Islands](19147.md): The Marshall Islands voted to condemn Hamas at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-auto4-39]

-  [Micronesia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federated_States_of_Micronesia): Micronesia voted to condemn Hamas at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-auto4-39]

  &#x20;In June 2025, Micronesia voted against a resolution calling for a ceasefire at the United Nations.

  [^cite_note-\:29-397]

-  [Nauru](21302.md): The government condemned the attacks and the "taking of innocent civilians as hostages", further calling on the international community to "stand in solidarity and prayer with the State of Israel," while also supporting "Israel's right to defend itself."

  [^cite_note-398]

  &#x20;In June 2025, Nauru voted against a resolution calling for a ceasefire at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-\:29-397]

-  [New Zealand](4913064.md): Foreign Minister [Nanaia Mahuta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nanaia_Mahuta) expressed deep concern at the outbreak of violence between Israel and Gaza. She called for the immediate halt to violence, the protection of all civilians, and the upholding of international humanitarian law.

  [^cite_note-The_Spinoff_NZ_leaders-399]

  &#x20;Prime Minister [Chris Hipkins](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chris_Hipkins) subsequently stated that New Zealand unequivocally condemned Hamas' terror attacks, stating that the target of civilians and hostage taking violated fundamental international humanitarian principles. Hipkins also stated Israel had the right to defend itself.

  [^cite_note-400]

  &#x20;On 25 October Carolyn Schwalger, New Zealand's Permanent Representative to the United Nations, delivered a statement to the [United Nations Security Council](31956.md) calling for a "humanitarian pause" to allow Gazan civilians to receive aid and for the creation of safe zones.

  [^cite_note-401]

  &#x20;By 18 November, New Zealand had contributed a total of NZ\$10 million to support humanitarian activities by the [International Committee of the Red Cross](5023832.md) and [World Food Programme](33582.md) in Israel and the Palestinian Territories.

  [^cite_note-402]

-  [Palau](23372.md): President [Surangel Whipps Jr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Surangel_Whipps_Jr.) wrote to Israeli Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu where he condemned the attack by Hamas on Israel, calling it an "act of terror", further adding Palau's solidarity with Israel.

  [^cite_note-403]

  &#x20;In June 2025, Palau voted against a resolution calling for a ceasefire at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-\:29-397]

-  [Papua New Guinea](22943.md): Prime Minister [James Marape](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Marape) condemned the attacks in Israel. He also called on Hamas and other militant groups to resolve matters in relation to Israel through dialogue and called on Israel to consider its response to the attacks.

  [^cite_note-404]

  &#x20;In June 2025, Papua New Guinea voted against a resolution calling for a ceasefire at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-\:29-397]

-  [Solomon Islands](265083.md): Solomon Islands voted to condemn Hamas at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-auto4-39]

-  [Tonga](30158.md): Tonga voted to condemn Hamas at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-auto4-39]

  &#x20;In June 2025, Tonga voted against a resolution calling for a ceasefire at the United Nations.

  [^cite_note-\:29-397]

-  [Tuvalu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tuvalu): Tuvalu voted to condemn Hamas at the [United Nations](31769.md).

  [^cite_note-auto4-39]

  &#x20;In June 2025, Tuvalu voted against a resolution calling for a ceasefire at the United Nations.

  [^cite_note-\:29-397]

## International organizations

-  [African Union](10634933.md): [African Union Commission](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/African_Union_Commission) Chairperson [Moussa Faki](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moussa_Faki) expressed his "utmost concern" at the situation and called for an immediate cessation of hostilities, additionally, he recalled that denial of the fundamental rights of the Palestinian people, particularly that of an independent and sovereign State, was the "main cause of the permanent Israeli-Palestinian tension."

  [^cite_note-405]

-  [Arab League](52625.md): The League said "Israel's continued implementation of violent and extremist policies is a time bomb depriving the region of any serious opportunity for stability in the foreseeable future."

  [^cite_note-\:3-184]

-  [ASEAN](106539.md): On 20 October, the bloc released a statement calling for an immediate end to violence and urged both parties to assure the security of civilians and create a humanitarian corridor. It also urged the international community to pursue a peace process between Israel and Palestine. It reaffirmed its "support for a negotiated two-State solution" to the Israeli–Palestine dispute.

  [^cite_note-406]

- [BRICS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BRICS): In a virtual summit chaired by South African President Cyril Ramaphosa, the grouping denounced attacks on civilians in Palestine and Israel, with many leaders calling the forced displacement of Palestinians, within Gaza or outside the territory, "war crimes."

  [^cite_note-407]



Gathering in support to Israel in front of the European Parliament in Brussels in presence of [Roberta Metsola](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roberta_Metsola), [Ursula von der Leyen](2991932.md), [Charles Michel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Michel) and members of the Parliament, 11 October 2023

-  [European Union](9317.md): President of the European Commission [Ursula von der Leyen](2991932.md) said she "unequivocally" condemned "the attack carried out by Hamas terrorists against Israel", calling it "terrorism in its most despicable form" and saying "Israel has the right to defend itself against such heinous attacks".

  [^cite_note-408]

  [^cite_note-\:4-112]

  &#x20;EU ambassador to Israel [Dimiter Tzantchev](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dimiter_Tzantchev) condemned the attack.

  [^cite_note-409]

  &#x20;Israeli flags were raised outside the headquarters of the [European Commission](9974.md) and the [European Parliament](9581.md) in [Brussels](3708.md), while the latter's president [Roberta Metsola](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roberta_Metsola) led a vigil in the chamber on 11 October to commemorate the Israeli victims of the attack which also featured a minute's silence and a rendition of the [Israeli national anthem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hatikvah). On 9 October, ahead of the EU council meeting convened to define a common EU position, EU Enlargement Commissioner [Oliver Varhelyi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oliver_Varhelyi) announced on X that the European Commission would be suspending immediately all payments due in developmental aid to Palestine.

  [^cite_note-410]

  &#x20;The consensus emanating from the 9 October ministers meeting was a resolution condemning attacks by Hamas but also calling "for the protection of civilians and restraint, the release of hostages, for allowing access to food, water and medicines to Gaza in line with international humanitarian law".

  [^cite_note-411]

  &#x20;The announcement by Varhelyi was criticized by the foreign ministers of several EU members state such as Spain, Ireland, Belgium and Luxembourg who insisted that only individual countries could make such decisions. The bloc corrected Varhelyi's announcement and said that it would review payments instead to prevent misuse.

  [^cite_note-landmark-257]

  &#x20;In the [United Nations General Assembly](31957.md) vote on [the resolution calling for](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_General_Assembly_Resolution_ES-10/21) an immediate and sustained humanitarian truce and cessation of hostilities, eight EU states voted in favour, four against and 15 abstained.

  [^cite_note-ap-20231028-412]

  &#x20;Ursula von der Leyen and other European leaders were reluctant to impose [sanctions on Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boycott,_Divestment_and_Sanctions) despite clear violations of international law.

  [^cite_note-413]

  &#x20;On 26 January 2024, the EU Commission and the High Representative issued a joint communication endorsing the International Court of Justice's order on South Africa's request for the indication of provisional measures in regard of the [Application of the Convention on the Prevention and Punishment of the Crime of Genocide in the Gaza Strip (South Africa v. Israel)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/South_Africa_v._Israel_\(Genocide_Convention\)), noting that "Orders of the International Court of Justice are binding on the Parties and they must comply with them".

  [^cite_note-414]

-  [NATO](21133.md): Spokesperson Dylan White said that the bloc condemned "terrorist attacks by Hamas against NATO partner Israel" and that "Israel has the right to defend itself".

  [^cite_note-415]

  &#x20;In the United Nations General Assembly vote on [the resolution calling for](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_General_Assembly_Resolution_ES-10/21) an immediate and sustained humanitarian truce and cessation of hostilities, nine NATO member states voted in favour, four against and 18 abstained.

  [^cite_note-unvr-20231027-416]



On 27 October, the [United Nations General Assembly](31957.md) passed [Resolution ES-10/21 calling for](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_General_Assembly_Resolution_ES-10/21) an "immediate and sustained" humanitarian truce and cessation of hostilities.

-  [United Nations](31769.md): The United Nations Special Coordinator for the Middle East Peace Process [Tor Wennesland](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tor_Wennesland) condemned the attack, stating that the "events have resulted in horrific scenes of violence and many Israeli fatalities and injuries, with many believed to be kidnapped inside the Strip". He also urged that the "heinous attacks targeting civilians must stop immediately".

  [^cite_note-417]

  &#x20;The [United Nations Interim Force in Lebanon](787766.md) said it was enhancing its presence near the border with Israel, including "counter rocket-launching operations".

  [^cite_note-f24live-178]

  &#x20;[UN High Commissioner for Human Rights](22463.md) [Volker Türk](71721726.md) criticized Israel's imposition of a [total siege on Gaza](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/October_2023_Israeli_blockade_of_the_Gaza_Strip), saying that such a move was "prohibited under international law".

  [^cite_note-418]

  &#x20;UN Secretary-General [António Guterres](471806.md) condemned the actions of Hamas, but said he was "deeply distressed" by Israel's decision to impose a total blockade on the Gaza Strip.

  [^cite_note-419]

  &#x20;On 27 October 2023, the [United Nations General Assembly](31957.md) passed [a resolution calling for](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_General_Assembly_Resolution_ES-10/21) an immediate and sustained humanitarian truce and cessation of hostilities, adopted by a vote of 121 states to 14, with 44 abstentions.

  [^cite_note-un-20231027-15]

  &#x20;On 13 November, [UN flags](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UN_flag) in offices in [Bangkok](56615.md), [Tokyo](30057.md) and [Beijing](18603746.md) were set at half-mast while staff observed a minute's silence in memory of employees killed in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-420]

  &#x20;On 27 March, [Francesca Albanese](72566699.md), the [United Nations Special Rapporteur on the occupied Palestinian territories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Special_Rapporteur_on_the_occupied_Palestinian_territories), stated that there are "reasonable grounds" to believe that Israel is committing genocide in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-421]

  &#x20;In a report by investigators from the UN's Commission of Inquiry, Israel and Hamas have been accused of committing war crimes and human rights abuses since 7 October 2023.

  [^cite_note-422]

  &#x20;In November 2024, the United Nations Special Committee, wrote in a report covering the period to July 2024: "Through its siege over Gaza, obstruction of humanitarian aid, alongside targeted attacks and killing of civilians and aid workers, despite repeated UN appeals, binding orders from the International Court of Justice and resolutions of the Security Council, Israel is intentionally causing death, starvation and serious injury," (...) "using starvation as a method of war and inflicting collective punishment on the Palestinian population" (...) "consistent with the characteristics of genocide".

  [^cite_note-423]

  [^cite_note-424]

- In 2023, the [Prosecutor of the International Criminal Court](33903993.md), [Karim Ahmad Khan](11606957.md), stated that "the attacks against innocent Israeli civilians on [Oct. 7](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Hamas-led_attack_on_Israel) represent some of the most serious international crimes that shock the conscience of humanity, crimes which the [ICC](14880.md) was established to address". He noted that Israel has a "robust system intended to ensure compliance with [international humanitarian law](1093604.md)", and reiterated the importance of letting [humanitarian aid](434679.md) into Gaza.

  [^cite_note-425]

  &#x20;On 20 May 2024, on the advice of a panel of ICC experts, Khan applied for arrest warrants for Israeli Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu and Hamas's leader in Gaza, Yahya Sinwar. He also applied for arrest warrants for two other Hamas members, [Ismail Haniyeh](41207463.md) and [Mohammed al-Masri](92534.md), and the Israeli defence minister Yoav Gallant.

  [^cite_note-426]

- In May 2024, judges of the [International Court of Justice](14918.md) called on Israel to halt its intended military offensive in the city of Rafah.

-  [UNICEF](19698110.md): On 13 November, UNICEF stated more than 700,000 children in Gaza were [displaced](477535.md).

  [^cite_note-427]

- [Non-Aligned Movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Non-Aligned_Movement): On 19 January, the bloc criticised Israel's war on Gaza at a summit in [Kampala](56689.md) and adopted a resolution calling for a ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-428]

### Political internationals and transnational political alliances

- [Liberal International](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liberal_International): The bureau of the network of liberal parties worldwide said it stood "in solidarity with the people of Israel" and condemned the "attacks against civilians in the south of the country and recognises Israel's right to defend itself". It also said the Hamas's attacks "do not serve the interest of the Palestinian people."

  [^cite_note-429]

  - [Renew Europe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Renew_Europe) said it was "horrified by the senseless terrorist attacks against the people of Israel and stand with them at this difficult time. There is no justification for barbaric attacks against innocent civilians."

    [^cite_note-430]

  - The [Alliance of Liberals and Democrats for Europe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alliance_of_Liberals_and_Democrats_for_Europe_Party) said it was asserting its "firm and unyielding support for Israel in these critical moments. Israel, a pivotal democratic ally, not only possesses the right but is also obligated under international law to safeguard its citizens against such acts of terror."

    [^cite_note-431]

- The [Progressive Alliance of Socialists and Democrats](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Progressive_Alliance_of_Socialists_and_Democrats) said it was "horrified by the terrorist attacks Hamas has committed against innocent Israelis" and called for Hamas to end its violence and immediately release all those it kidnapped.

  [^cite_note-432]

  - The [Party of European Socialists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Party_of_European_Socialists) announced its "solidarity to all Israelis" and condemned "this large-scale, indiscriminate, assault on citizens and communities", while calling on "Hamas to stop the attacks immediately. Terrorism is never the answer."

    [^cite_note-433]

- The [Greens–European Free Alliance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Greens%E2%80%93European_Free_Alliance) condemned "the violent terrorist attacks by Hamas in the strongest terms," and called "for an immediate stop to this violence" and renewed "efforts towards deescalation and peace".

  [^cite_note-434]

- [Centrist Democrat International](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Centrist_Democrat_International) expressed "its resounding rejection of terrorism as a means of political action" and condemned "the cruel attack by Hamas against the people of Israel".

  [^cite_note-435]

  [^cite_note-436]

  - The [European People's Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_People's_Party) strongly condemned the "atrocious attack against Israel" and said "the country has a right to defend itself & its citizens from violent terrorist actions which achieve nothing but devastating loss of life, division & destruction."

    [^cite_note-437]

- The [International Democracy Union](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Democrat_Union) expressed "full solidarity with the State of Israel and its people" and said "Israel has the right to defend itself against these terrorist attacks and has our full support."

  [^cite_note-438]

  - The [European Conservatives and Reformists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Conservatives_and_Reformists_Group) strongly condemned "the unprecedented terror attacks on Israel coming out of Gaza, including the execution-style killings and kidnappings of Israeli civilians and soldiers" and said "the violence perpetrated by Hamas shows that one cannot negotiate with terrorists."

    [^cite_note-439]

- The [Identity and Democracy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Identity_and_Democracy) group condemned the attack, expressed "unwavering solidarity with Israel and its people" and reaffirmed Israel's right to defend itself from terror attacks.

  [^cite_note-440]

## Political parties



The [Labour Party (UK)](19279158.md) under [Keir Starmer](18681491.md) suspended several parliamentary candidates and MPs for comments they made about Israel.

### Africa

 [South Africa](17416221.md)

- The [Democratic Alliance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Democratic_Alliance_\(South_Africa\)) condemned "Hamas' unprovoked attack" and urged an "immediate end to violence" and return to negotiations to end the conflict.

  [^cite_note-441]

  [^cite_note-442]

- The [Economic Freedom Fighters](40005536.md) expressed their support for Hamas and pledged to send arms to Hamas should they win the 2024 general election.

  [^cite_note-443]

  [^cite_note-444]

- The [Patriotic Alliance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patriotic_Alliance)

  [^cite_note-445]

  [^cite_note-446]

  &#x20;and the [Freedom Front Plus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Freedom_Front_Plus)

  [^cite_note-447]

  &#x20;stated their support for Israel.

### Americas

 [Brazil](3383.md)

- The [Socialism and Liberty Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Socialism_and_Liberty_Party) condemned the attacks as a genocide.

  [^cite_note-448]

- The [Workers' Cause Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Workers'_Cause_Party) provided "unwavering support" to Hamas and stated that "the regime imposed by the Israelis in Palestine is no less brutal than the [Nazis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nazism) themselves".

  [^cite_note-449]

  [^cite_note-450]

- The [Workers' Party](224622.md) referred to the Israeli actions in Gaza as [genocide](12441.md) while stating that the Palestinian militants' attack on civilian areas was "unacceptable".

  [^cite_note-451]

 [United States](3434750.md): The [Green Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green_Party_of_the_United_States) announced its condemnation of attacks on civilians and demanded an immediate ceasefire.[^cite_note-452]

### Asia

 [Bangladesh](3454.md)

- The [Workers Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Workers_Party_of_Bangladesh), [Jatiya Samajtantrik Dal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jatiya_Samajtantrik_Dal), [Ganatantri Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ganatantri_Party), [National Awami Party (Muzaffar)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Awami_Party_\(Muzaffar\)), and other pro-[Awami League](404861.md) leftist political parties staged demonstrations altogether in support of Palestine. In a march under the banner of the 'Bangladesh Peace Council' on 12 October, leaders of these parties criticized the Foreign Ministry's initial statement as 'duplicity' and 'ambivalent'. They also termed the *Operation Al-Aqsa Deluge* as a 'defensive operation' and compared it to the [Bangladesh Liberation War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bangladesh_Liberation_War)

- The [Bangladesh Jamaat-e-Islami](21739288.md) organized a rally denouncing the Israeli assault on Gaza and calling upon the Muslim world to safeguard the well-being and rights of the Palestinian people. The party orchestrated a procession in [Dhaka](56656.md), during which its leaders expressed strong condemnation towards Israel for cutting off power and water supply to Gaza.

  [^cite_note-453]

 [Syria](7515849.md): The rebel anti-Assad [Syrian National Army](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Syrian_National_Army) (SNA), previously the [Free Syrian Army](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_Syrian_Army) (FSA), expressed sympathy for the Palestinian people in the Gaza Strip, comparing Israel's attacks in Gaza to attacks on rebel-held areas in Syria's [Idlib Governorate](7445043.md).[^cite_note-syria-gaza-454]

 [Turkey](11125639.md): The [Kurdistan Workers' Party](69680.md) (PKK) condemned the Israeli actions as "genocide".[^cite_note-455][^cite_note-456]

### Europe

 [Bosnia and Herzegovina](3463.md)

- The [Croatian Democratic Union of Bosnia and Herzegovina](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Croatian_Democratic_Union_of_Bosnia_and_Herzegovina) condemned Hamas' attacks on Israel as well as justifications and relativisations of Hamas' terrorist acts by certain politicians in Bosnia and Herzegovina.

  [^cite_note-457]

- The [Party of Democratic Action](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Party_of_Democratic_Action) condemned the killings of innocent people and called on the United Nations and other international actors to help resolve the Palestinian issue through dialogue. They also asked for a cessation of the "violence of the Israeli military over the civilians in Gaza, non-selective rocket attacks, the announcement of a ground invasion and the requests for dislocation of more than a million of Palestinians in Gaza".

  [^cite_note-458]

 [United Kingdom](31717.md): The [Labour Party](19279158.md) under [Keir Starmer](18681491.md) suspended several parliamentary candidates and MPs, including [Graham Jones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Graham_Jones_\(politician\)), [Andy McDonald](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andy_McDonald_\(politician\)), [Azhar Ali](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Azhar_Ali_\(politician\)) and [Kate Osamor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kate_Osamor), for comments they made about Israel. Jones said Britons who go to Israel to fight for the [Israel Defense Forces](39237.md) "should be locked up".[^cite_note-459] Osamor wrote that there was an "international duty" to remember the victims of the Holocaust and that "more recent genocides in [Cambodia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cambodian_genocide), [Rwanda](676659.md), [Bosnia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bosnian_genocide) and now [Gaza](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Allegations_of_genocide_in_the_2023_Israeli_attack_on_Gaza)" should also be remembered.[^cite_note-460]

### Oceania

 [New Zealand](4913064.md)

- [New Zealand National Party](333149.md) leader [Christopher Luxon](62303596.md) and [ACT New Zealand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ACT_New_Zealand) leader [David Seymour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Seymour_\(New_Zealand_politician\)) condemned Hamas for attacking Israeli civilians.

- [Green Party of Aotearoa New Zealand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green_Party_of_Aotearoa_New_Zealand)'s foreign affairs spokesperson [Golriz Ghahraman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golriz_Ghahraman) condemned the targeting of civilians and urged both Palestinian armed groups and Israeli forces to protect civilians.

  [^cite_note-The_Spinoff_NZ_leaders-399]

- On 18 October, [Te Pāti Māori](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Te_P%C4%81ti_M%C4%81ori) (Māori Party) advocated the expulsion of the Israeli ambassador if Israel did not implement a ceasefire or open a humanitarian corridor in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-461]

## Islamist groups

-  [Hezbollah](13919.md): The group congratulated Hamas and praised the attack as a response to "Israeli crimes", adding that the militants had "divine backing". It also said its leadership in [Lebanon](17771.md) was in contact with Hamas about the operation.

  [^cite_note-ap1-462]

  &#x20;On 11 October, the group said that "sending aircraft carriers to the region will not frighten the resistance factions ready for confrontation until victory is achieved", referring to the deployment of two US carrier strike fleets to the eastern Mediterranean.

  [^cite_note-463]

  &#x20;Hezbollah warned that a [ground invasion of the Gaza Strip](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_invasion_of_the_Gaza_Strip_\(2023%E2%80%93present\)) could result in a wider conflict in the Middle East.

  [^cite_note-464]

-  [Houthi movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Houthi_movement): The group's Ansarullah Political Bureau stated that "[Operation Al-Aqsa Flood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operation_Al-Aqsa_Flood) revealed the weakness, fragility, and impotence of the temporary Zionist entity while showing the strength and effectiveness of the resistance in Palestine and its ability to strike the Israeli depth".

  [^cite_note-\:0-465]

  &#x20;It also said that the [Yemenis](350939.md) stood with the Palestinians and resistance movements explaining that they were ready to participate in the battle to defend Palestine and the nation's sanctities against the "usurping" Zionist entity.

  [^cite_note-\:0-465]

  &#x20;Houthi leader [Abdul-Malik al-Houthi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abdul-Malik_al-Houthi) warned that any [intervention in Gaza by the United States](65284610.md) would result in [a Houthi intervention](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Houthi_involvement_in_the_Israel%E2%80%93Hamas_war).

  [^cite_note-466]

- [Islamic State](9087364.md) [Al-Shabaab](8726463.md): The group congratulated the Palestinian militants, saluting "all the brave heroes, the brave commandos, and all those stationed in the Holy Land". It also called on Muslims to wage [Jihad](16203.md) against "Jews and their allies".

  [^cite_note-467]

  [^cite_note-468]

-  [AQIM](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AQIM) and [JNIM](53401088.md): Two branches of [al-Qaeda](1921.md), [al-Qaeda in the Islamic Maghreb](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Qaeda_in_the_Islamic_Maghreb) and [Jama'at Nasr al-Islam wal Muslimin](53401088.md), released a joint statement praising the attacks on Israel and encouraging Hamas and other Palestinian militant groups to inflict further violence on Jews.

  [^cite_note-469]

- [Islamic State](9087364.md) [Islamic State](9087364.md) (commonly known as ISIS or Daesh): The group released instructions on its official newspaper [Al-Naba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Naba), on "practical steps to fight the Jews" worldwide and "support Muslims in Palestine", including "targeting [Jewish temples](48559.md)".

  [^cite_note-\:10-470]

  [^cite_note-471]

  &#x20;However, it also criticized Hamas for its narrowed focus on Israel and connections to Iran.

  [^cite_note-\:10-470]

-  [Pakistani Taliban](18953699.md): The group urged Muslims worldwide to aid Palestinians and called Hamas' attack on Israel "extremely joyous and encouraging news about the oppressed Muslim [Ummah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ummah)".

  [^cite_note-472]

-  [Tahrir al-Sham](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tahrir_al-Sham): The group's clerics held prayers for a Palestinian victory and said it celebrated Hamas' [jihad](16203.md) while condemning its ties with [Shiite](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shiite)-led Iran.

  [^cite_note-473]

  &#x20;The group expressed support for the right of the Palestinian people to reclaim their land.

  [^cite_note-syria-gaza-454]

## Charities and NGOs

- Indonesian NGO Medical Emergency Rescue Committee (MER-C) condemned Israel after reporting that Israel had targeted its hospital in Gaza and killed one of its personnel.

  [^cite_note-indonesia-474]

- Following the Israeli airstrike on the [Indonesian Hospital](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesia_Hospital), [Doctors Without Borders](20498.md) urged "all parties to respect health infrastructures".

  [^cite_note-475]

- [Mercy Malaysia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mercy_Malaysia), in solidarity with Palestine, confirmed it was monitoring the situation in Gaza and was exploring opportunities to provide aid in the form of health assistance, psychosocial support, and medical supplies.

  [^cite_note-476]

- The [Egyptian Red Crescent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Egyptian_Red_Crescent_Society) sent more than two tons of medical supplies to the Gaza Strip.

  [^cite_note-477]

- The [Uyghur Human Rights Project](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uyghur_Human_Rights_Project) stated that it "condemns in the strongest terms possible the barbaric attacks by Hamas terrorists on innocent Israelis".

  [^cite_note-\:11-478]

- The [World Uyghur Congress](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Uyghur_Congress) released a statement condemning "horrific attacks by Hamas against Israeli civilians".

  [^cite_note-\:11-478]

  [^cite_note-479]

- The [Jewish Federations of North America](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_Federations_of_North_America) announced a \$500 million fundraising campaign to help Israel.

  [^cite_note-480]

- A [Doctors Without Borders](20498.md) video shared by [Amnesty International](18947898.md) head [Agnès Callamard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Agn%C3%A8s_Callamard) stated, "This brutal annihilation of an entire populations health system stretches beyond what humanitarian aid can fix."

  [^cite_note-481]

  &#x20;On 4 December, [Red Cross](15487.md) president [Mirjana Spoljaric Egger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mirjana_Spoljaric_Egger) visited the Gaza Strip, stating, "the things I saw there are beyond anything that anyone should be in a position to describe."

  [^cite_note-482]

  &#x20;On 10 December, Bushra Khalidi, an expert with [Oxfam](19698439.md), stated the situation was no longer "just a catastrophe, it's apocalyptic."

  [^cite_note-483]

- On 27 December, an MSF representative stated, "You absolutely cannot depict this as a humanitarian response: When we cannot guarantee the safety of our teams".

  [^cite_note-484]

  &#x20;Mairav Zonszein, a [Crisis Group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Crisis_Group) analyst, stated, "It is clear Israel's war objective is not eradicating Hamas, but eradicating the ability to live in Gaza."

  [^cite_note-485]

- [Gisha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gisha_\(human_rights_organization\)), an Israeli human rights organization, stated, "The catastrophic situation today must be understood within the context of Israel's pre-October 7 policies, including vis-a-vis Palestinians' freedom of movement between Gaza, Israel, and the West Bank".

  [^cite_note-486]

## Corporations



Pro-Palestinian protest in [Ottawa](22219.md), Canada, 23 November 2023

- [Adani Ports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adani_Ports) stated that it was still operating its port in [Haifa](55606.md) but would "monitor the situation".

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Amazon](90451.md) CEO [Andy Jassy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andy_Jassy) contacted families of employees in Israel and will provide relief. They also planned operations to keep AWS services online in the country.

  [^cite_note-auto2-488]

- [American Express](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_Express) sent \$1.5 million in donations to help the relief efforts in Israel while also keeping close contact with staff in the region.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Bank of America](347756.md) closed its [Tel Aviv](31453.md) office.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Bristol-Myers Squibb](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bristol-Myers_Squibb) stated that it would continue to maintain medical supplies for patients in the region.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Citigroup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Citigroup) has allowed for workers in Israel to work at home until the situation was secured.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Carnival Cruises](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carnival_Cruises) stated that it would not stop at Israeli ports and would adjust accordingly.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Decathlon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Decathlon_\(retailer\)) stated that deliveries in Israel may be suspended or delayed.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Eli Lilly and Company](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eli_Lilly_and_Company) stated it would monitor the situation and would continue to provide medical care for its patients in Israel.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [FedEx](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FedEx) initially suspended services to Israel but later relaunched service on 12 October.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Goldman Sachs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Goldman_Sachs) asked its employees in Tel Aviv to work from home until the security situation cleared.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Google](1092923.md) CEO [Sundar Pichai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sundar_Pichai) stated that it had made contact with its over 2,000 employees in the area and was working to support them as well as humanitarian and relief organizations on the ground.

  [^cite_note-auto2-488]

- [H\&M](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/H\&M) temporarily closed all of its stores in Israel.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [InterContinental Hotels Group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/InterContinental_Hotels_Group) stated that they would add security to their hotels in Israel that remained open, while closing the [Six Senses Shaharut](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Six_Senses_Shaharut?action=edit\&redlink=1) and the [Hotel Indigo Tel Aviv](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hotel_Indigo_Tel_Aviv?action=edit\&redlink=1).

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [JPMorgan Chase](231001.md) asked its employees in Israel to work for home until the security situation cleared. CEO [Jamie Dimon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jamie_Dimon) provided his support for Israel.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

  [^cite_note-auto2-488]

- [McDonald's](2480627.md) franchisees in the Middle East said it would donate \$1 million to be split evenly between the Red Cross and the World Food Program to help victims of the conflict. McDonald's operators in Kuwait and Pakistan made donations towards humanitarian relief in Gaza, while [McDonald's Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/McDonald's_Israel) offered discounts to soldiers and other members of the security forces and gave out 100,000 free meals.

  [^cite_note-489]

  &#x20;However, McDonald's Corporation later stated that all decisions were made independently from the company. It also rejected all claims of "disinformation" over its stance in the conflict and said the decisions were done by the franchisees independently without any consent or approval from McDonald's themselves.

  [^cite_note-490]

- [Morgan Stanley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morgan_Stanley) asked its employees in Israel to work from home until the security situation cleared.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Norwegian Cruise Lines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Norwegian_Cruise_Lines) announced it would redirect ships away from Israeli ports of call in October.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Nvidia](39120.md) cancelled an AI summit in [Tel Aviv](31453.md) that would have featured CEO [Jensen Huang](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jensen_Huang).

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Oracle Corporation](22591.md) condemned the attacks against Israel by Hamas. It also pledged to support the Israeli Government and defense establishments.

  [^cite_note-companies-491]

- [Paramount](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paramount_Global) condemned the attacks and donated to humanitarian aid.

  [^cite_note-492]

  &#x20;The company also cancelled the [MTV Europe Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV_Europe_Music_Awards) which was scheduled to be held in [Paris](22989.md) on 5 November, saying that the conflict in Israel and Gaza "does not feel like a moment for a global celebration" as well as France's high-security airport alerts.

  [^cite_note-493]

- [Puma](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Puma_\(brand\)) suspended store operations in Tel Aviv but continued deliveries in some cities.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Royal Caribbean](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_Caribbean_Group) announced it would make adjustments to replace Israeli ports of call.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [Sesame Workshop](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sesame_Workshop) released a statement stating "Our hearts are with Israeli and Palestinian children and families affected by the crisis in the region. All children deserve a safe childhood free from violence and terror."

  [^cite_note-494]

- [Starbucks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Starbucks) sued the [Workers United](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Workers_United) union, alleging that a pro-Palestinian social media post from the Starbucks Workers United account angered customers and damaged its reputation. The company demanded that the union cease using its name and logo, while the union filed a countersuit saying that they were defamed by Starbucks for implying that it supported terrorism and violence.

  [^cite_note-495]

- [Tesla](5533631.md) announced that all 17 superchargers in Israel would be free until further notice.

  [^cite_note-auto2-488]

- [Teva Pharmaceuticals](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teva_Pharmaceuticals), the largest maker of generic drugs who is also based in Tel Aviv stated that production would not be affected and that backup production would continue to run.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [UBS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UBS) told its Israeli staff to work from home and banned employees from travelling to the Middle East.

  [^cite_note-496]

- [UPS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Parcel_Service) suspended service to Israel.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

- [The Walt Disney Company](37398.md) stated that it would provide humanitarian support to Israelis.

  [^cite_note-497]

- [Web Summit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_Summit) CEO [Paddy Cosgrave](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paddy_Cosgrave) expressed shock at the rhetoric of Western governments in response to the Israeli bombardment and said that "war crimes are war crimes even when committed by allies, and should be called out for what they are". In response, several tech companies such as Google, [Meta](7529378.md), Amazon and [Intel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Intel) announced that they would pull out of the upcoming Web Summit to be held in [Lisbon](18091.md) in November 2023. Cosgrave later apologized and resigned on 21 October.

  [^cite_note-498]

- [WWE](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/WWE) renamed a 2025 episode of its *[WWE NXT](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/WWE_NXT)* television show, initially titled "NXT Invasion", to "[NXT Showdown](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NXT_Showdown)" as the event fell on the second anniversary of the October 7 attacks.

  [^cite_note-499]

- [Zara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zara_\(retailer\)) temporarily closed all of its stores in Israel.

  [^cite_note-auto3-487]

### Flight cancellations

- [Aegean Airlines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aegean_Airlines) suspended flights to Tel Aviv on 9 October.

- [Air Canada](145623.md) suspended all flights to [Tel Aviv](31453.md) from [Toronto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toronto_Pearson_International_Airport) and [Montreal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Montr%C3%A9al%E2%80%93Trudeau_International_Airport) through 31 October.

  [^cite_note-500]

- [Air France](14998012.md) suspended its flights to Tel Aviv indefinitely.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Air India](26457880.md) suspended its flights to Tel Aviv from 13 to 18 October.

- [American Airlines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_Airlines) suspended flights to Tel Aviv.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [British Airways](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/British_Airways) suspended all flights to Tel Aviv on 11 October even though it had been operating during the outbreak of fighting.

- [Brussels Airlines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brussels_Airlines) cancelled all flights to Tel Aviv.

- [Cathay Pacific](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cathay_Pacific) cancelled its flights to Tel Aviv from [Hong Kong](13404.md) on 10 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Delta Air Lines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delta_Air_Lines) cancelled all flights to Tel Aviv through 31 October, issuing refunds for affected passengers.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [EasyJet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/EasyJet) suspended its flights to Tel Aviv on 9 and 10 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Emirates](142715.md) suspended flights to Tel Aviv on 9 October.

- [Etihad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Etihad) cancelled its flights to Tel Aviv from [Abu Dhabi](18950756.md) on 10 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Hainan Airlines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hainan_Airlines) cancelled its flights to Tel Aviv from [Shanghai](27643.md) on 9 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Iberia](98613.md) cancelled the majority of its flights to Tel Aviv on 8 October, maintaining a single flight between [Madrid](41188263.md) and Tel Aviv starting 10 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [ITA Airways](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ITA_Airways) cancelled all flights to Tel Aviv on 8 October.

- [KLM](16882.md) cancelled a planned evacuation of Dutch citizens after it could not receive insurance.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Korean Air](315411.md) cancelled its flights to Tel Aviv from [Seoul](19159283.md) on 9 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [LOT Polish Airlines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LOT_Polish_Airlines) cancelled all flights to Tel Aviv on 8 October.

- [Lufthansa](97733.md) cancelled all flights to and from Tel Aviv on 9 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Norwegian Air](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Norwegian_Air) cancelled a planned evacuation flight from Tel Aviv to [Oslo](22309.md) after it could not receive insurance.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Ryanair](262878.md) suspended flights to Tel Aviv on 9 October.

- [Swiss International Air Lines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Swiss_International_Air_Lines) stated it would not operate any flights to and from Israel through 14 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [TAP](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TAP_Air_Portugal) cancelled its flights to and from Tel Aviv on 9 and 10 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Transavia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Transavia) cancelled all flights to Tel Aviv from Paris and Lyon.

- [United Airlines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Airlines) suspended all flights to Tel Aviv after running two flights on 7 and 8 October.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

- [Wizz Air](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wizz_Air) cancelled its flights to Tel Aviv until further notice.

  [^cite_note-auto1-501]

## Celebrities, political figures and other personalities

See also: [Gaza war protests § Artists](75044900.md#Artists)

### Celebrities, other personalities

- [Gal Gadot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gal_Gadot), who is Israeli, condemned the attack on her home country.

  [^cite_note-502]

- [Gigi Hadid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gigi_Hadid), who is of Palestinian descent, wrote on Instagram: "While I have hopes and dreams for Palestinians, none of them include the harm of a Jewish person. The terrorizing of innocent people is not in alignment with & does not do any good for the 'Free Palestine' movement. The idea that it does has fueled a painful, decades-long cycle of back\&forth retaliation."

  [^cite_note-503]

- [Bella Hadid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bella_Hadid), who is of Palestinian descent, posted a written statement on Instagram mourning the loss of Israeli and Palestinian lives.

  [^cite_note-504]

  &#x20;She condemned "terrorist attacks on any civilians, anywhere". She also expressed concern over the [humanitarian situation in Gaza](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_humanitarian_crisis_\(2023%E2%80%93present\)).

  [^cite_note-505]

  &#x20;Hadid posted on Instagram: "I've been sent hundreds of death threats daily, my phone number has been leaked, and my family has felt to be in danger."

  [^cite_note-506]

  [^cite_note-507]

  &#x20;In November 2023, Israeli music duo Ness & Stilla released the single "[Harbu Darbu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harbu_Darbu)",

  [^cite_note-508]

  &#x20;which called for Bella Hadid's death alongside that of [Dua Lipa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dua_Lipa) and [Mia Khalifa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mia_Khalifa).

  [^cite_note-509]

- [Justin Bieber](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Justin_Bieber) shared a post on Instagram asking people to "pray for Israel" which actually depicted the aftermath of the [Israeli bombing of the Gaza Strip](76139149.md).

  [^cite_note-510]

- [Kris Jenner](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kris_Jenner) and her daughter [Kylie Jenner](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kylie_Jenner) announced that they support Israel and were saddened by the loss and damage done by Hamas in Israel.

  [^cite_note-511]

- [Quentin Tarantino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quentin_Tarantino) expressed support for Israel. In mid-October 2023, he visited an IDF base in southern Israel to "boost the morale" of [Israeli troops](39237.md).

  [^cite_note-512]

- [Madonna](142056.md) expressed deep support for Israel and condemned the violent actions against Israeli citizens. She expressed hope that the conflict will be resolved through peaceful means and not violence. Madonna reiterated that the attack was the action of Hamas and not all Palestinians.

  [^cite_note-513]

- [U2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U2) changed the lyrics to "[Pride (In the Name of Love)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pride_\(In_the_Name_of_Love\))" to honor those killed in the [Re'im music festival massacre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Re'im_music_festival_massacre).

  [^cite_note-514]

  [^cite_note-515]

- [Greta Thunberg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Greta_Thunberg) expressed support for Gaza and Palestine, and called for a "ceasefire, justice and freedom for Palestinians and all civilians affected".

  [^cite_note-516]

  [^cite_note-517]

  [^cite_note-518]

- [Salman Rushdie](28722.md) expressed his "horror" at both Hamas' attack and Israel's retaliation and called for a "cessation in hostilities".

  [^cite_note-519]

- [Fazıl Say](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Faz%C4%B1l_Say) said his upcoming performances with the [City of Birmingham Symphony Orchestra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/City_of_Birmingham_Symphony_Orchestra) in Switzerland were cancelled after he called for Benjamin Netanyahu to "stand trial for war crimes, genocide and massacres".

  [^cite_note-520]

- [Vince Offer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vince_Offer), who is Israeli, expressed outrage at US president Joe Biden's slow responses in preventing Hamas' attack on Israel and the [Russian invasion of Ukraine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine).

  [^cite_note-521]

- [Rupi Kaur](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rupi_Kaur) declined an invitation to attend a [Diwali](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diwali) celebration hosted by US Vice President [Kamala Harris](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kamala_Harris) in the [White House](33057.md), citing the Biden administration's continued support of Israel and its bombardment of Gaza, which she called "the collective punishment of a trapped civilian population".

  [^cite_note-522]

- [Douglas Murray](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Douglas_Murray_\(author\)) expressed support for Israel's actions in the Gaza Strip.

  [^cite_note-523]

  [^cite_note-524]

  &#x20;On 28 January 2024, Murray interviewed Israeli Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu.

  [^cite_note-525]

- [Moataz Matar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moataz_Matar) had his UK visa cancelled by the Home Office and was placed on a watchlist barring him from reentry after he was accused of supporting Hamas following his participation in a pro-Palestine protest in London.

  [^cite_note-526]

- [Ana Tijoux](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ana_Tijoux) participated at a benefit concert in [Santiago, Chile](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Santiago,_Chile) to raise funds for hospitals in the Palestinian territories and express solidarity with civilians in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-527]

- [Elissa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elissa_\(singer\)) expressed her solidarity with Palestinians by posting a tweet featuring [Palestine's flag](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flag_of_Palestine) with a quote "if you cannot lift the injustice, at least tell everyone about it".

  [^cite_note-528]

- [Jamie Lee Curtis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jamie_Lee_Curtis) expressed support for Israel.

  [^cite_note-529]

- [Summer Walker](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Summer_Walker) condemned the "genocide and execution of innocent lives" in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-530]

- Former [Pink Floyd](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pink_Floyd) bassist and co-founder [Roger Waters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roger_Waters), a longtime and controversial critic of Israel,

  [^cite_note-Thorpe-2013-531]

  &#x20;described Hamas' attack on Israel as being "blown out of all proportion by the Israelis," prompting the president of the [Central Israelite Committee of Uruguay](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_Israelite_Committee_of_Uruguay), [Roby Schindler](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roby_Schindler?action=edit\&redlink=1), to accuse him of antisemitism and urging the local [Sofitel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sofitel) branch not to host him in his scheduled concert in the country in November.

  [^cite_note-532]

- [Kehlani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kehlani) posted her solidarity with Palestine on Instagram and expressed disapproval towards other celebrities had remained silent on the matter.

  [^cite_note-533]

- [Melissa Barrera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Melissa_Barrera) described the war as "genocide and ethnic cleansing" on Instagram and compared the situation in Gaza to "a concentration camp", prompting [Spyglass Media Group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spyglass_Media_Group), the producers of the upcoming film *[Scream VII](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scream_VII)* to announce her removal from the cast, citing "zero tolerance for antisemitism or the incitement of hate in any form, including false references to genocide, ethnic cleansing, Holocaust distortion or anything that flagrantly crosses the line into hate speech."

  [^cite_note-apdrop-534]

- [Susan Sarandon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Susan_Sarandon) joined a pro-Palestine protest in New York City on 17 November in which she was accused of antisemitism after chanting "From the river to the sea, Palestine will be free". She also criticized conflations of antisemitism with criticism of Israel and expressed her opposition to antisemitism as well as Islamophobia. The [United Talent Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Talent_Agency) later said it no longer represented her.

  [^cite_note-apdrop-534]

- [Rage Against the Machine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rage_Against_the_Machine) frontman, [Zack de la Rocha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zack_de_la_Rocha), attended a pro-Palestinian protest in Washington, D.C.

  [^cite_note-535]

- [Genesis Owusu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Genesis_Owusu) called for a ceasefire and a "Free Palestine" in his acceptance speech at the [2023 ARIA Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_ARIA_Music_Awards).

  [^cite_note-536]

- [Hend Sabri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hend_Sabri) resigned as Goodwill Ambassador for the [World Food Programme](33582.md) in protest over what she called the use of "famine and siege as weapons of war" in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-537]

- [Olly Alexander](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olly_Alexander), who represented the United Kingdom at the [Eurovision Song Contest 2024](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eurovision_Song_Contest_2024),

  [^cite_note-538]

  &#x20;signed a letter by LGBT association Voices4London, which accused Israel of committing apartheid against Palestinians and "[Zionist propaganda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hasbara)" of [pinkwashing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pinkwashing_\(LGBT\)#Israel) the Israeli occupation of Palestinian territories.

  [^cite_note-539]

- [David Clennon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Clennon) expressed his support for Palestine. He said via email: "Hollywood used to be unanimous in showing its admiration and loyalty to Israel.
